<div class="section--wide">
<div class="container feature feature--content-left">
<div class="feature-content">
<h2 class="sme-v3-h1 sme-v3-center-justify-sm">Business Valuation Calculator</h2>
<p class="sme-v3-largetext">
At SMERGERS, we define Business Valuation as a technique used to capture the true value of a business based on similar comparable companies. Our comparable data includes publicly trading companies across all stock exchanges in the world and private transactions of thousands of small businesses on SMERGERS, which makes our valuation estimate as precise as it can get. Curious to know the valuation of your business? Try our simple, yet one of the most effective, online valuation calculators out there for free.
</p>
<p class="sme-v3-extra-margin-top">
<a class="btn btn-success btn-lg" href="/how-to-value-a-business/">Try Valuation Tool</a>
</p>
</div>
<a data-analytics-event="click" data-analytics-event-name="Clicked Home Page View Valuation Tool Image" href="/how-to-value-a-business/">

</a>

<div class="section--wide" style="background-color: #fafafa;">
<div class="container feature feature--content-right">
<div class="feature-content">
<h2 class="sme-v3-h1 sme-v3-center-justify-sm">Industry Watch</h2>
<p class="sme-v3-largetext">
SMERGERS Industry Watch offers a bird’s eye view of traditional and sunrise sectors which includes Finance, IT, Auto, Media, Engineering, Pharma, Food &amp; Beverage, Healthcare, E-commerce, Internet companies and many other industries that are at an inflection point and transforming the global economy. Our infographics are easy to comprehend as we present you with only the most relevant metrics in a clutter free and visually appealing format.
</p>
<p class="sme-v3-extra-margin-top">
<a class="btn btn-success btn-lg" href="/industry-watch/">Explore Industry Watch</a>

</a>
</div>
</div>
<div class="section--wide">
<div class="container feature feature--content-left">
<div class="feature-content">
<h2 class="sme-v3-h1 sme-v3-center-justify-sm">SMERGERS Guide</h2>
<p class="sme-v3-largetext">
Our experience in the industry, collaborating and working closely with small business owners &amp; investors across the globe, led us to create SMERGERS Guide for the benefit of small business sellers and buyers. Consider this your go-to resource when buying and selling a small business.
</p>
<p class="sme-v3-extra-margin-top">

<div id="id-faqs-wrapper" style="border-top:1px solid #eee;">
<div class="faq-section" id="id_ABOUT_PAGE_FAQ" style="display: block;">
<div class="container">
<div class="row">
<div class="faq-wrapper">
<div class="faq">
<div class="faq-title col-md-2">
<h3>
Frequently<br/>
Asked<br/>
Questions
</h3>
</div>
<ul class="faq-questions sme-v3-no-margin-top col-md-10">
<li style="margin-bottom:1em;">
<div class="faq-question-item">Is SMERGERS an investment bank?</div>
<div class="answer-item"><p>SMERGERS is an investment banking platform which aims to remove all the clutter and make quality introductions between businesses, investors, acquirers, lenders and advisors, extending beyond their geographical location.</p></div>
</li>
<li style="margin-bottom:1em;">
<div class="faq-question-item">Is SMERGERS a ‘business for sale’ website?</div>
<div class="answer-item"><p>Yes and no. Yes, because we have sell side profiles of businesses which are seeking a takeover. And no, because we are not a classifieds website, but a private network of members.</p></div>
</li>
<li class="faq-show-more" style="margin-bottom:1em;">
<div class="faq-question-item">What is Business Valuation?</div>
<div class="answer-item"><p>Business valuation is process whereby a fair value of a company is arrived at using one or more of the valuation methodologies.</p></div>
</li>
<li class="faq-show-more" style="margin-bottom:1em;">
<div class="faq-question-item">Can businesses / investors from any country register on SMERGERS?</div>
<div class="answer-item"><p>SMERGERS is a global platform with businesses and acquirers / investors from across the globe. Businesses and investors from any country or industry can register on SMERGERS. The only pre-requisite is that the business needs to be a registered entity with the Government and should be able to produce proof of the same when required and investors should be working in a company or if self employed must be able to produce proof of business.</p></div>
</li>
<li class="faq-show-more" style="margin-bottom:1em;">
<div class="faq-question-item">Should I use SMERGERS instead of an advisor?</div>
<div class="answer-item"><p>You should use SMERGERS to gain qualified leads from large network of investors, buyers, lenders and advisors from across the globe. You may also want to connect with suitable advisors on SMERGERS who can help negotiate and close your deal.</p></div>
</li>
<p><a class="faq-show-more-button" href="#">Show more</a></p>
</ul>
<div class="clear"></div>
